### Rule
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-caf97a1f]]#atom-technical-atom-36e2d85a1dff3d90 Characters can reduce the damage they receive from monsters by means of armor. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01283))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-caf97a1f]]#atom-technical-atom-2640ab7439ba915d When making a defense roll, if your roll is double ones, that means that you've been hit extremely hard. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01289))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-caf97a1f]]#atom-technical-atom-1610cb13d9bf24d5 When making a defense roll, criticals do not occur like they do with a strike roll. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01291))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-112572a5]]#atom-technical-atom-a71fb6908aeedeb1 The defender doesn't even have to roll the dice. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01325))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-112572a5]]#atom-technical-atom-69d6505c34ac6971 If the defender's roll is double sixes , the attack will always fail. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01328))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-112572a5]]#atom-technical-atom-a9ee5b97c7b7c5e1 The attacker must make a strike roll to determine their base damage, then add their bonus damage. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01332))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-351dfe08]]#atom-technical-atom-68c6fe0a0136f544 Ducard II, with a life force of -4 , must make a death check . _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01383))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-351dfe08]]#atom-technical-atom-e3a5d42d5426123f Characters with 0 or negative life force must make a death check every hour . _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01387))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-351dfe08]]#atom-technical-atom-60caf840338650d8 In one hour , they must make another death check. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01392))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-351dfe08]]#atom-technical-atom-1b6efc39f5e3d37d If left untreated, they must make another death check after 1 hour , and _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01404))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-3064cdd410b6191b Also, even if you search all over Alecrast, you will usually only find one with a required strength up to 5 lower than u _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01414))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-05281fafb2abcbe0 However, if you want one of higher quality, you must go to a larger town. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01415))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-40130b2b0317db5b Whenever an adventurer declares that their attack on a monster is a mercy, that monster will always remain alive. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01418))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-4b8299c066ab8214 Magical weapons can deal damage to monsters that normal weapons cannot harm. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01434))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-c939199d146a433f Mithril silver weapons can deal damage to monsters that normal weapons cannot harm. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01450))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-c4de255d7bd2633c Mithril silver weapons and armor are never , if ever, placed in shops or traded for money. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01453))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-f90d1675d8ef38c2 A rune master can use all magic up to their skill level, but cannot use magic of a higher level than their current skill _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01503))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-5deed1ceb398ed64 If the result is anything other than double ones, the magic takes effect. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01526))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-bcbd2d14cdfb2152 When attempting to cast resistible magic on a monster, the caster must determine whether their opponent can resist it. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01528))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-3a65fc9f4593e1b3 If the 2D roll comes up double sixes , the magic automatically succeeds and will take its full effect, no matter how hig _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01529))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-8e16a0f219ac7b6d Fire Bolt is resistible magic, so if it's resisted by the opponent, its effect will be reduced. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01532))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-babf748b4792da09 In this case, the adventurer (the player controlling them) must make a success roll. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01533))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-e700f4b3d6040b60 If the adventurer's final score ( mental power resistance + 2D ) is equal to or greater than the monster's magic intensi _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01546))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-eca2e4bc850a3026 *In this way, when making a resistible magic check, you can also call it a successful resist or failed resist from the p _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01548))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-2829b73ce041980f If the score of the one casting the magic is greater, the magic will take full effect, but if there is a tie or if the s _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01560))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-275eeed980b17b44 When the one casting the magic rolls double ones , the magic takes no effect at all. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01562))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-80e1b7afc5b974bd If there are multiple targets in the path of the lightning bolt, you must make a check for each one's attempt to resist. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01570))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-9e3539f24a0f778c The player rolls the dice four times and for each one must determine whether the magic breaks through the resist and tak _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01571))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-b9e1f9e98279a547 When rolling the dice, you must clearly declare which target the dice are meant for before you actually roll. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01577))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-8aa3aac3eaf3e60b Each resisting side must then make a mental power resistance roll, using that determined final score as the target score _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01585))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-a63d0d9dbcfafdef If the side casting the magic rolls double sixes, no one can resist, except for those who also roll double sixes. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01588))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-7ceff197ed36fbf4 If the one on whom the magic is being cast chooses not to resist the magic, it will definitely take effect so long as th _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01593))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-d7f9876ad034f60b If a character's mental power reaches 0 , they will lose consciousness . _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01603))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-3b6fb3860ac6c730 Even if magic fails , such as by rolling double ones or being resisted by the opponent, a character must still consume t _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01604))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-7f4db1b26de382bc If you expand a spell that affects one enemy (or ally), it can instead affect multiple at the same time. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01619))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-1570abe425d8d4ff Y ou can also increase it by a fraction, such as x1.5, in which case the fraction is rounded up when determining the mul _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01644))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-7e08be4a9b5e11a5 You can also increase it by a fraction, such as x1.5, in which case the fraction is rounded up when determining the mult _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01647))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-3ad9da2c71631729 you can make a damage check (strike roll) twice or three times . _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01650))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-288a012b020e9db7 There's some magic whose effects are negated or reduced if they're resisted by the target, but will also let you increas _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01653))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-cf96e17fe321650f you can add a +1 or +2 bonus to your final score. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01654))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-73e7e64793512047 If you roll 10 or greater during the strike roll, a critical will occur and deal even more damage, same as in weapon com _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01683))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-5a4242ede56ed466 Most magic of the damage type can still deal damage even if it's resisted by the target, only with a reduced effect. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01684))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-8a9cecb5ae7d3856 In this case, no matter how much magic power your character (monster) has, they cannot deal damage to the target at all. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01685))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-ceecf7c37172d7ce Also, even magic of the damage type may in rare cases take no effect if resisted. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01686))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-1d3df87d4518b8db When casting any magic, no matter what kind of magic it is, you must roll 2D to check whether an automatic failure (doub _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01691))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-e0f45121bb64a67e However, if you feel this is cumbersome, or if you forget to make a note, then when it becomes necessary (nullifying mag _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01693))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-ba5ce375989f9d54 When you use magic that nullifies other magic, if your roll is double sixes , the magic will automatically be nullified, _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01694))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-b35c1b3e6c7d09c4 When certain magic is cast against other magic, it can cancel the effect of the prior magic but still take its own effec _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01702))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-b93ece26ca92cb00 In the case of magic that does not take effect if resisted, if the target does resist it, the magic will not take its ef _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01705))_
- [[sword-world-rpg-complete-edition-section-chapter-3-3-4-4-actions-you-can-take-while-standing-still-attacks-from-monsters-against-characte-6f861318]]#atom-technical-atom-62ed6c6165ea8d85 ⑤ If the target successfully resisted in ④ , the new magic cast will not take effect. _(Sword World RPG - Complete Edition.pdf (source-range-e5870dca-01712))_
